	id = ITA_declare_the_winner
	icon = GFX_USA_hands_votes
	cost = 0.4
	ai_will_do = { factor = 1 }
	prerequisite = {
		focus = ITA_reaffirm_the_independence_of_the_armed_forces

		focus = ITA_under_the_carpet
	}
	x = 2
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = ITA_reaffirm_the_independence_of_the_armed_forces
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus ITA_declare_the_winner"

		remove_ideas = {
			ITA_uprooting_fascism
			ITA_government_of_national_unity
		}

		add_ideas = {
			ITA_turbulent_democracy
			ITA_king_umberto_ii
		}

		# Italy has just held its first election in forty years, so the franchise
		# catches up with the fact. It starts the rework at no voting; this lands
		# on registered voting from either of the rungs below it and never
		# overshoots into universal suffrage, which the tree sells separately.
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = tno_vote_franchise_no_voting }
			tno_vote_franchise_improve = yes
			tno_vote_franchise_improve = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_idea = tno_vote_franchise_elite_voting }
			tno_vote_franchise_improve = yes
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = ITA_camera_natunity_mode

			country_event = {
				id = ITA_med.7004
			}
		}
	}
}
